## `AGO-6430-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Mutual-Fund-NAV Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Mutual/Hedge Fund Quantitative Algorithmic Execution Trader
- **Intro:** Mutual-Fund-NAV Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Mutual/Hedge Fund Quantitative Algorithmic Execution Trader — System Clock Synchronization Check: Clock skew must be ≤ 50 milliseconds (ms) from the NTP server time. If skew exceeds 50 ms, the system must not proceed to live trading.
- **Key tolerance:** Clock skew must be ≤ 50 milliseconds (ms) from the NTP server time. If skew exceeds 50 ms, the system must not proceed to live trading.
- **Standards:** `ISO/IEC 27001:2022`, `ISO 31000:2018`, `IOSCO Principles for the Regulation of Collective Investment Sch`, `No. 1`
